- Consumes: Task 3's matcher and render; Task 2's catalog templates; Task 5's `TlsMode`.
- Produces: the working `parse` and `looks_like_connection` (R21).
  - Recognizer order: (a) native parsers (`postgres://`/`postgresql://` URI including multi-host, the libpq DSN, `mysql://`/`mariadb://`, `jdbc:` stripped and re-routed, SQLite paths/`file:` URIs/`:memory:`); then (b) the catalog templates of the preferred engine first, then of the others.
  - Mapping: `sslmode`/`ssl-mode`/`sslMode`/`useSSL` → `MappedTo::TlsMode`, with `prefer` → Auto, `disable` → Disable, `require` → Require, `verify-ca` → VerifyCa, `verify-full` and MySQL `VERIFY_IDENTITY` → VerifyFull. Also `application_name` → `ApplicationName`, `connect_timeout` → `ConnectTimeout`, SQLite `mode=ro` → `ReadOnly(true)`, and PostgreSQL `options` → `PgOptions` (kept as extra).
  - Passwords go to `secrets` and never into `fields`.
  - Input is trimmed. Input over 64 KiB is rejected as `TooLong`.

```rust
// tests/url_roundtrip.rs
#[test] fn every_catalog_template_compiles_and_round_trips() {
    for spec in EngineCatalog::embedded().engines() { for ct in &spec.conn_types { if ct.template.is_empty() { continue; }
        let t = compile(&ct.template, &ct.id).unwrap();
        for fields in sample_fields(spec.kind, &t) {  // 50 generated samples per template incl. IPv6, non-ASCII db, '@' in user
            let url = render(&t, &fields, &[], RenderTarget::Copy).unwrap(); let o = ok(parse(&url, Some(spec.kind)));
            assert_eq!(o.fields.values, fields.values, "{url}");
        } } }
}
```

### Task 8: Credential files and auth providers (HV-SEC-03, -04)

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/core/hacivat-secrets/src/{files/{mod,pgpass,pg_service}.rs, providers.rs}`
- Create: `crates/core/hacivat-secrets/tests/{pgpass,pg_service,providers}.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 4's `SecretStore`.
- Produces:
  - `pgpass_path`: `$PGPASSFILE`, then `%APPDATA%\postgresql\pgpass.conf`, then `~/.pgpass`.
  - `pgpass_lookup`: libpq-exact. It is case-sensitive, the first match wins, `\:` and `\\` are escapes, `*` is a wildcard, the fifth field is the rest of the line, `localhost` also matches a Unix socket, and the port defaults to 5432. A file with `mode & 0o077 != 0` is ignored with a warning.
  - `read_pg_service`: `PGSERVICEFILE`, then `~/.pg_service.conf`, then `PGSYSCONFDIR/pg_service.conf`. The `password` key goes to `PgService::password`, under the same permission rule.
  - `resolve_auth` in this order: draft, then pgpass (when the provider is `PgPass`), then the store for the retention, then `NeedsPrompt`. `None` gives `NotNeeded`. A failure is never retried with another source.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** (HV-SEC-04 vectors):
```rust
#[test] fn pgpass_vectors() {
    let f = file(0o600, "db.example:5432:app:alice:s3cr\\:et\n*:*:*:bob:pw\n");
    assert_eq!(hit(&f, "db.example", 5432, "app", Some("alice")).password.expose_secret(), "s3cr:et");
    assert_eq!(hit(&f, "db.example", 5432, "app", None).user.as_deref(), Some("alice"));
    assert!(pgpass_lookup(&f, "db.example", 5432, "app", Some("carol")).unwrap().is_none());
    assert!(pgpass_lookup(&f, "DB.EXAMPLE", 5432, "app", Some("alice")).unwrap().is_none());
    assert_eq!(hit(&file(0o600, "h:5432:d:u:pass:with:colons\r\n"), "h", 5432, "d", Some("u")).password.expose_secret(), "pass:with:colons");
    assert_eq!(hit(&file(0o600, "h:5432:d:u:\n"), "h", 5432, "d", Some("u")).password.expose_secret(), "");
    #[cfg(unix)] assert_eq!(pgpass_lookup(&file(0o644, "h:5432:d:u:p\n"), "h", 5432, "d", Some("u")).unwrap_err(), PgPassWarning::Permissions { path: last_path(), mode: 0o644 });
}
#[test] fn pg_service_sections() { let s = read_pg_service(&file(0o600, "[orders]\nhost=db\nport=5433\ndbname=orders\nuser=deploy\npassword=pw\n[x]\nhost=h\n")).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(s.iter().map(|x| x.name.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), ["orders", "x"]); assert_eq!(s[0].line, 1);
    assert_eq!(s[0].fields, vec![("host".into(),"db".into()),("port".into(),"5433".into()),("dbname".into(),"orders".into()),("user".into(),"deploy".into())]);
    assert_eq!(s[0].password.as_ref().unwrap().expose_secret(), "pw"); }
#[test] fn resolution_order() {
    let (store, _) = mem_store(); store.put(&k(), sec("stored"), Retention::Forever).unwrap();
    assert!(matches!(resolve_auth(&req(AuthProviderId::Password, Some(&sec("draft"))), &store, &files()), AuthOutcome::Ready(AuthMaterial { source: SecretSource::Draft, .. })));
    assert!(matches!(resolve_auth(&req(AuthProviderId::Password, None), &store, &files()), AuthOutcome::Ready(AuthMaterial { source: SecretSource::Store(Retention::Forever), .. })));
    let (empty, _) = mem_store(); assert!(matches!(resolve_auth(&req(AuthProviderId::Password, None), &empty, &files()), AuthOutcome::NeedsPrompt { ref user } if user == "deploy"));
    assert!(matches!(resolve_auth(&req(AuthProviderId::PgPass, None), &empty, &files_with_pgpass("h:5432:d:deploy:pp\n")), AuthOutcome::Ready(AuthMaterial { source: SecretSource::PgPass { line: 1, .. }, .. })));
    assert!(matches!(resolve_auth(&req(AuthProviderId::PgPass, None), &empty, &files_with_pgpass("other:5432:d:deploy:pp\n")), AuthOutcome::Failed(AuthError::PgPassNoMatch)));
    assert!(matches!(resolve_auth(&req(AuthProviderId::None, None), &empty, &files()), AuthOutcome::Ready(AuthMaterial { source: SecretSource::NotNeeded, password: None, .. })));
}
```

  - `crates/app/hacivat-app/src/settings_ui/mod.rs` (the preview call)
  - `locales/en/hacivat-ui.ftl`: line 118 only, the preview's doc line `$number (Number)` becomes `$number (Decimal)` (an allowed exception to append-only, P20); `locales/tr/hacivat-ui.ftl`: no change
  - `locales/en/hacivat-core.ftl` (block `m2a-t10-errors`)
- Create: `locales/tr/hacivat-core.ftl` (header plus block `m2a-t10-errors`), `crates/core/hacivat-driver/catalog/errors.toml`, `crates/core/hacivat-driver/tests/{classify,error_fluent_golden}.rs`, `crates/core/hacivat-driver/tests/golden/error-conn.{en,tr}.txt`, `xtask/tests/fixtures/l10n/l026/**`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1's `ErrClass`, `RawError` and `FixId`.
- Builds `hacivat-l10n` and `hacivat-app` in the shared gpui `target`, alongside T9: run `cargo clean -p hacivat-l10n -p hacivat-app` before final verification.
- Produces:
  - **`errors.toml`.** One `[[class]]` per `ErrClass::ALL` with `id` and `reachable`. 26 classes are reachable in M2a: all `network.*`; all `tls.*`; `auth.{invalid,expired,locked,method_unsupported}`; `authz.database`; `config.{invalid,unknown_database}`; `server.{too_many_connections,starting,shutdown,read_only,unsupported_version}`; `cancelled`; and `internal`.
  - **Code tables:**
    - PostgreSQL: `28P01` → auth.invalid; `28000` → authz.database; `3D000` → config.unknown_database; `53300` → server.too_many_connections; `57P03` → server.starting; `57P01`/`57P02` → server.shutdown; `25006` → server.read_only; `08001`/`08006` → network.reset.
    - MySQL/MariaDB: `1045` → auth.invalid; `1862`/`1820` → auth.expired; `3118` and MariaDB `4151` → auth.locked (P44); `1049` → config.unknown_database; `1040` → server.too_many_connections; `1290`/`1792` → server.read_only; `1044` → authz.database; `2059`/`2061` → auth.method_unsupported.
    - SQLite: `14` (CANTOPEN) → config.unknown_database; `26` (NOTADB) → config.invalid; `8` (READONLY) → server.read_only; `5`/`6` (BUSY/LOCKED) → internal.
  - **Transport rules:** `Dns` → network.dns; refused, timed out, reset and unreachable → the matching `network.*`; `Tls(UnknownIssuer|Expired|NotYetValid)` → tls.untrusted; `NameMismatch` → tls.hostname; `Protocol` → tls.protocol; `ClientCertRequired` → tls.client_cert_required; `TlsNotOffered` → tls.not_supported_by_server (P9); `Timeout` → network.timeout; `Cancelled` → cancelled.
  - The fragile-text step is skipped unless `lang_hint` is `None` or `en`.
  - **`fixes_for`** (normative):
    - auth.invalid → `[ReenterPassword, EditConnection]`
    - network.dns → `[TryAgain, EditConnection]`
    - other network.* → `[TryAgain, EditConnection]`
    - tls.untrusted and tls.hostname → `[EncryptWithoutVerifying, EditConnection]`
    - config.unknown_database → `[UseDatabase, EditConnection]`
    - server.unsupported_version → `[SupportedVersions, EditConnection, ConnectAnyway]`
    - server.too_many_connections and server.starting → `[TryAgain, EditConnection]`
    - config.invalid → `[]`
    - everything else → `[EditConnection]`
  - **Fluent:** `error-conn-<class>-title` and `-body` in `hacivat-core.ftl` for every reachable class, in EN and TR (TR marked `# Review: PO`). Variables: `$name`, `$host`, `$port`, `$user`, `$db`, `$engine`, `$version`, `$file`; all are `(String)`, including `$port` and `$version`, so a port never gets grouping separators (P20). The mock's five texts are used verbatim; Task 10 writes EN copy for the other 21 reachable classes, in the same voice.
  - **L026** in `xtask l10n check`: a reachable class without both English messages is an error.
  - **`(Decimal)`:** the variable type in `hacivat-l10n-schema` and the codegen; it generates `crate::args::ExactDecimal`, with `From<ExactDecimal> for ArgValue` in `args.rs`. `xtask`'s smoke match and L013 handle it. The settings regional preview becomes `(Decimal)` and uses its typed ID instead of `t_dynamic` (R15).
  - **`hacivat_l10n::search::PreparedQuery`** (R15, moved from Task 18, P14): `PreparedQuery::new(query: &str, o: &SearchOpts)` and `matches(&self, key: &SearchKey) -> Option<Match>`, equivalent to `find`. Task 18's explorer filter consumes it.

### Task 11: Docker integration harness and the minimal CI job (R17)

**Files:**
- Create:
  - `docker/m2a/{compose.yml, targets.toml, README.md}`
  - `docker/m2a/init/{pg.sql, mysql.sql}`, `docker/m2a/pg-tls/init.sh`, `docker/m2a/pg-tr/Dockerfile`
  - `xtask/src/docker.rs`
  - `crates/core/hacivat-testkit/tests/targets.rs`
- Modify: `crates/core/hacivat-testkit/src/lib.rs`, `xtask/src/main.rs` (the `docker` arm, appended), `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (the `docker-pr` job, appended)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1's `hacivat-testkit` signatures.
- Produces:
  - **Compose project.** `compose.yml` sets `name: hacivat-m2a`, and every service has `container_name: m2a-<id>` (e.g. `m2a-pg17`) (P25).
  - **Services.** Each is bound to `127.0.0.1` and has a healthcheck. Host ports:
    - `pg13` (postgres:13, the unsupported fixture) 54313
    - `pg14` 54314, `pg15` 54315, `pg16` 54316, `pg17` 54317, `pg18` 54318
    - `pg17-tls` 54327 (self-signed, `ssl=on`)
    - `pg17-tr` 54337 (`lc_messages=tr_TR.UTF-8`, built from `pg-tr/Dockerfile`, which adds the locale)
    - `mysql80` 33080, `mysql84` 33084
    - `mariadb106` 33106, `mariadb1011` 33111, `mariadb114` 33114
  - **Credentials** everywhere: user `hv_test`, password `hv_test_pw`, database `hv_test`.
  - **Extra fixtures** on every server: the databases `orders` and `orders_archive`. MySQL and MariaDB also get the user `hv_locked` (`ACCOUNT LOCK`) and the user `hv_ro` (read-only grants).
  - **`targets.toml`.** One entry per service: `id`, `engine`, `major`, `port`, `tls_default` (`none`/`self_signed`, observed on first `up` and committed), and `sets`. Set `pr` = `pg17`, `mysql84`. Set `full` = all.
  - **`cargo xtask docker up|down|status|test --set pr|full [-p <crate>…]`.**
    - `up` waits until every selected service is healthy (timeout 180 s), then copies `mysql84`'s `/var/lib/mysql/ca.pem` to `<main checkout>/target/docker/mysql84-ca.pem`. Only the controller runs `up` and `down` (Global Constraints, Docker).
    - `test` **never runs `up` or `down`** (P25). When a selected service is not healthy, it fails fast and names the unhealthy services. Otherwise it runs `cargo test -p hacivat-driver-pg -p hacivat-driver-mysql -p hacivat-driver-sqlite -p hacivat-session -- --ignored` (or only the given `-p` crates) with `HACIVAT_DOCKER=1`, `HACIVAT_DOCKER_SET=<set>` and `HACIVAT_DOCKER_ARTIFACTS=<main checkout>/target/docker`. The main checkout is found from `git rev-parse --git-common-dir`, so a worktree resolves the same directory.
  - **`hacivat-testkit`:**
    - `docker_enabled_from(v: Option<&str>) -> bool` is true only for `Some("1")`; `docker_enabled()` is `docker_enabled_from(std::env::var("HACIVAT_DOCKER").ok().as_deref())`. No test sets or removes an environment variable (P25).
    - `targets` filters by `HACIVAT_DOCKER_SET`.
    - `artifact(name: &str) -> PathBuf` is `$HACIVAT_DOCKER_ARTIFACTS/<name>`, and panics with a clear message when the variable is unset (it is only called from Docker tests).
  - **The `docker-pr` CI job** (ubuntu-latest), on PR and push: `cargo test -p hacivat-driver --features fake`, then `cargo xtask docker test --set pr`.

### Task 12: Profiles: model, `connections.toml` store, field mapping, naming rules, app model (HV-SEC-05 profile split, D-004 rules)

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/core/hacivat-session/src/profile/{mod,store,fields,rules}.rs`, `crates/app/hacivat-app/src/connections/{model,boot}.rs`, `crates/app/hacivat-app/tests/support/mod.rs` (helpers `shell_with_connections` and `settle`, appended), `locales/{en,tr}/hacivat-ui.ftl` (block `m2a-t12-profiles`)
- Create: `crates/core/hacivat-session/tests/profiles.rs`, `crates/app/hacivat-app/tests/connections_model.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 3's `render`; Task 7's `parse` outcome; Task 4's `SecretStore`; Task 1's `ProfileDraft::{new, edit}`, `HintSource` and `ConnectionManager` signatures (real behaviour arrives with Task 16).
- Builds in the shared gpui `target` (it touches `hacivat-app`); T16 builds `hacivat-session` in its own `target-core/t16`.
- Produces:
  - **The store.**
    - TOML: `version = 1`, then `[[connection]]` tables with keys `id`, `name`, `engine`, `environment`, `read_only`, `host`/`port` or `socket` or `file`/`create_if_missing`, `database`, `user`, `auth`, `retention`, `tls.mode`, `tls.ca_file`, `tls.client_cert`, `tls.client_key`, `transport`, `connect_timeout_s`, `keepalive_s`, `application_name`, `max_connections`, `extra` (an array of `{ key, value }`), `origin`, `allow_unsupported_server`.
    - Writes are atomic (`hacivat_settings::fsio::atomic_write`), with the file at 0600 and the directory at 0700.
    - Unknown keys are preserved through toml_edit.
    - A `password`, `passwd` or `pwd` key is dropped on load with `SecretKeyIgnored` and never written back.
    - A version above 1 makes the store `read_only`.
    - An unparsable file goes through `fsio::quarantine(path, "corrupt", now)`.
  - **Field mapping:** `to_fields`, `apply_parse`, `display_url` and `copy_url`.
  - **Rules (R22, R23):** `suggest_name` (mock `suggestName`), `production_hint` (whole tokens, `HintSource`, P7), `duplicate_name`, `is_local_endpoint` and `needs_environment_prompt`. Host and name matching uses ASCII folding only; non-ASCII text is kept verbatim, never cased.
  - **`ConnectionsBoot::load`/`for_tests`**, **`boot::install`** and **`ConnectionsModel`**: profile CRUD with ordered writes (the `LatestWriter` pattern, its writes spawned on the `CoreRuntime` handle rather than the GPUI background executor, P48) and secrets per R8.
    - `boot::install(cx, boot)` sets the `ConnectionsModel` Global, then shows one toast per diagnostic kind in `LoadedProfiles` (P29): `hv-conn-file-quarantined` (`$file`) when `quarantined`; `hv-conn-file-entries-skipped` (`$count (Number)`) for `InvalidEntry`, `DuplicateId` and `UnknownEngine` together; `hv-conn-file-password-ignored` for `SecretKeyIgnored` (it never echoes the value); `hv-conn-store-read-only` for `NewerVersion`. It never touches the keyring.
    - `save` runs `SecretStore::put` and `change_retention` on the core runtime's blocking pool, then the store write. A `FellBackTo` result raises the `hv-conn-keyring-unavailable` toast once per session.
    - `delete` calls `manager.forget` and `secrets.delete_all`.
    - `duplicate` calls `secrets.copy_all` and pushes the `hv-conn-toast-dup` toast.
    - New strings: `hv-conn-keyring-unavailable`, `hv-conn-store-read-only`, `hv-conn-file-quarantined`, `hv-conn-file-entries-skipped`, `hv-conn-file-password-ignored`, `hv-conn-toast-dup`.
  - **Test helpers (appended to `tests/support/mod.rs`):** `shell_with_connections(cx, lang)` (a shell started with `ConnectionsBoot::for_tests` and a FakeDriver registry), and `settle(vcx, d)`: `vcx.executor().allow_parking()`, `std::thread::sleep(d)`, then `vcx.run_until_parked()`. FakeDriver delays and core work run on the real Tokio clock, so GPUI tests wait in real time (P22).

```rust
    let mut d = ProfileDraft::new(); let r = apply_parse(&mut d, outcome("postgres://deploy:s3cret@db-prod-us.acme.io:5433/orders?sslmode=verify-full&application_name=hv&foo=bar"), false);
    assert_eq!(d.profile.endpoint, ProfileEndpoint::Server { host: "db-prod-us.acme.io".into(), port: Some(5433) });
    assert_eq!((d.profile.database.as_deref(), d.profile.user.as_deref()), (Some("orders"), Some("deploy")));
    assert_eq!(d.password.as_ref().unwrap().expose_secret(), "s3cret"); assert_eq!(d.profile.tls.mode, TlsMode::VerifyFull);
    assert_eq!(d.profile.application_name, "hv"); assert_eq!(d.profile.extra, vec![("foo".into(), "bar".into())]);
    assert!(r.password_moved); assert_eq!(r.extras_kept, ["foo"]);
}
#[test] fn apply_parse_engine_rules() { let mut untouched = ProfileDraft::new();
    assert_eq!(apply_parse(&mut untouched, outcome("mysql://h/db"), false).engine, Some(EngineChange::Switched { from: DbmsKind::Postgres, to: DbmsKind::MySql }));
    let mut typed = ProfileDraft::new(); typed.touched.insert(FieldId::Host);
    let r = apply_parse(&mut typed, outcome("mysql://h/db"), false); assert_eq!(r.engine, Some(EngineChange::Mismatch { url: DbmsKind::MySql, form: DbmsKind::Postgres }));
    assert!(r.filled.is_empty()); assert_eq!(typed.profile.engine, DbmsKind::Postgres); }
```

### Task 13: PostgreSQL driver (`tokio-postgres`): stream injection, TLS, detection, probes

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/core/hacivat-driver-pg/src/{lib,connect,tls,probe}.rs`
- Create: `crates/core/hacivat-driver-pg/tests/{unit.rs, docker_pg.rs}`; the section `## m2a-t13 — hacivat-driver-pg` in `docs/decisions/0002-driver-crates.md` (P41)

**Interfaces:**
- Builds with `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=<main checkout>/target-core/t13` (P14). The Docker project is already up (the controller's); never run `docker up` or `down`.
- Consumes: Task 1's `Driver`/`RawConn` and the `PgDriver` stub; Task 5's `TlsParams` and `StreamFactory`; Task 2's `SemVerLoose`.
- Produces: the real `PgDriver` (`impl Driver`; replaces the Task 1 stub body).
  - **Connect.** It always uses `tokio_postgres::Config::connect_raw(factory.open(), tls)`. The TLS connector is our own `MakeTlsConnect` over `tokio-rustls` with `TlsParams.config` and `server_name`. It emits `TlsStarted` and `TlsDone`, and writes rustls failures into `TlsParams::failure`.
  - **sslmode** (P9): `ssl_mode(mode: TlsMode, plaintext_allowed: bool) -> SslMode`. Auto with `plaintext_allowed` → `Prefer`; Auto without it → `Require`, so no password is sent in plaintext to a public host; Require, VerifyCa and VerifyFull → `Require`; Disable → `Disable`, connected with `NoTls`. tokio-postgres's "server does not support TLS" error (library text, not server text) maps to `RawErrorKind::TlsNotOffered`.
  - **Startup options:** `application_name`; `options=-c default_transaction_read_only=on` when `read_only`; the sanitized extras.
  - **`server_info`:** `SHOW server_version_num`, `SELECT version()` and `pg_is_in_recovery()`.
  - **`tls_summary`:** `pg_stat_ssl` for `pg_backend_pid()`.
  - **`ping`:** the empty simple query `""`.
  - **`list_databases`:** `SELECT datname FROM pg_database WHERE datallowconn AND has_database_privilege(datname,'CONNECT') ORDER BY 1 LIMIT $1`.
  - **`probe`:** `Replica` when in recovery.
  - **`current_user`:** `SELECT current_user`.
  - **Errors:** `tokio_postgres::Error` becomes `RawError` with `sqlstate`, `severity`, `text`, `detail` and `hint`. A TLS failure becomes `RawErrorKind::Tls`; TLS required but not offered becomes `TlsNotOffered`. I/O errors become `Io(kind)`.
  - **Secrets (P49):** the password goes into `tokio_postgres::Config` only at connect time; `Config` keeps it as bytes (its `Debug` redacts it). The ADR section records this.
  - **`cancel_handle`:** `None` (M2b). The ADR records that `CancelToken::cancel_query_raw` accepts a stream from the same factory.

### Task 14: MySQL/MariaDB driver (`mysql_async`): the forwarder path, TLS, detection

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/core/hacivat-driver-mysql/src/{lib,connect,tls,probe}.rs`
- Create: `crates/core/hacivat-driver-mysql/tests/{unit.rs, docker_mysql.rs}`; the section `## m2a-t14 — hacivat-driver-mysql` in ADR 0002 (P41)

**Interfaces:**
- Builds with `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=<main checkout>/target-core/t14` (P14). The Docker project is already up (the controller's); never run `docker up` or `down`.
- Consumes: as Task 13, plus Task 5's `LoopbackForwarder` and `ForwarderMode`, Task 1's `MySqlDriver` stub, and Task 11's `hacivat_testkit::artifact`.
- Produces: the real `MySqlDriver` (`impl Driver`; registered for `MySql`, with MariaDB resolved through the fallback).
  - **Injection verdict (P10, settled by the pre-flight source read; record it in the ADR):** `mysql_async` 0.37 cannot run over a caller-provided stream (`Conn::new` always dials; its `Endpoint` holds a `TcpStream`). `caps.stream_injection = false`.
  - **`direct: None`** (non-Direct transports in M2b, and any connect with a `transport_wrap`, P13): run `LoopbackForwarder::bind(factory, private_dir, 1, ForwarderMode::Tcp)` and connect with `ip_or_hostname("127.0.0.1")`, `tcp_port(port)`, `prefer_socket(false)` and `SslOpts::with_danger_tls_hostname_override(tls_host)`, so TLS verifies the real name. The forwarder is **never** a Unix socket: `mysql_async` never applies TLS over a socket (R18). The forwarder's guard is pushed into the connection's guards. The connection reports that it was forwarded (`LiveConnection::forwarded`).
  - **`direct: Some(Target)`:** `mysql_async`'s own TCP: `ip_or_hostname(tls_host)`, `tcp_port(port)`, `resolved_ips(Some(<Endpoint::Network.resolved, as IpAddr>))`, `prefer_socket(false)`.
  - **TLS (ledger ruling, PO-away):** `pub fn ssl_opts(mode: TlsMode) -> Option<SslOpts>` builds the mode part; the connect path adds the roots, the client identity and the host-name override.
    - Disable → `None`.
    - Every other mode → `SslOpts::default().with_disable_built_in_roots(true)`, with root certificates from the CA file plus the OS roots from `rustls_native_certs::load_native_certs()`, each passed as DER (`PathOrBuf::Buf`) through `with_root_certs`. `webpki-roots` is compiled in but never consulted.
    - Require → `with_danger_accept_invalid_certs(true)` and `with_danger_skip_domain_validation(true)`.
    - VerifyFull → no danger flags.
    - VerifyCa → never reached: Validate rejects it for MySQL/MariaDB with `IssueCode::TlsModeUnsupported` (P11). `mysql_async` decides `skip_domain_validation` by searching the error text for `NotValidForName`, and the locked rustls 0.23.46 reports `NotValidForNameContext` ("certificate not valid for name …"), so a name mismatch is never skipped. M2b revisits this with an upstream fix or a `[patch]` fork. The ADR records the defect.
    - Client identity from PEM.
    - **Auto:** try TLS first. If the server lacks the SSL capability and `plaintext_allowed`, reconnect in plaintext. If not, `RawErrorKind::TlsNotOffered` (P9), which Task 10 maps to `tls.not_supported_by_server`.
  - **Other settings:** `prefer_socket(false)` on every connection (P10), so `mysql_async` never reconnects through `@@socket`. No local-infile handler is ever installed. `SET SESSION TRANSACTION READ ONLY` when `read_only`.
  - **Detection:** `SELECT @@version, @@version_comment, @@read_only, @@super_read_only` (MariaDB has no `super_read_only`; tolerate it) and `detect_mysql_family`.
  - **`tls_summary`:** `SHOW SESSION STATUS LIKE 'Ssl_version'` and `'Ssl_cipher'`.
  - **`list_databases`:** `SHOW DATABASES` minus the system schemas.
  - **`probe`:** `ServerReadOnly`, `SuperReadOnly`, `LowWaitTimeout` when `@@wait_timeout < 60`, and `LocalInfileOn` when `@@local_infile = 1`.
  - **`ping`:** `COM_PING`.
  - **Secrets (P49):** `Opts` stores the password as a `String` and derives `Debug`. Build `Opts` at the last moment, drop it right after the handshake, and never `Debug`-format `Opts`, `OptsBuilder` or `Conn`, in code or in tests.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests:**
```rust

    assert!(ssl_opts(TlsMode::Disable).is_none());
    let r = ssl_opts(TlsMode::Require).unwrap(); assert!(r.accept_invalid_certs()); assert!(r.skip_domain_validation()); assert!(r.disable_built_in_roots());
    let f = ssl_opts(TlsMode::VerifyFull).unwrap(); assert!(!f.accept_invalid_certs()); assert!(!f.skip_domain_validation()); assert!(f.disable_built_in_roots()); }

    assert_eq!(o.ssl_opts().unwrap().tls_hostname_override(), Some("db.acme.io")); }
```

  `opts_for` and `opts_for_forwarded` are `#[doc(hidden)] pub` functions that return the `mysql_async::Opts` the driver would connect with (direct, or to a forwarder port); the tests read its getters and never format it with `Debug`. The driver maps both rustls `NotValidForName` and `NotValidForNameContext` to `TlsFailure::NameMismatch`.

### Task 15: SQLite driver (`rusqlite` bundled)

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/core/hacivat-driver-sqlite/src/{lib,open,probe}.rs`
- Create: `crates/core/hacivat-driver-sqlite/tests/sqlite.rs`; the section `## m2a-t15 — hacivat-driver-sqlite` in ADR 0002 (P41)

**Interfaces:**
- Builds with `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=<main checkout>/target-core/t15` (P14).
- Consumes: Task 1's traits and the `SqliteDriver` stub.
- Produces: the real `SqliteDriver` (`impl Driver`; `caps.file_based = true`).
  - It opens on a dedicated blocking thread (`spawn_blocking`). The connection lives on that thread, reached through an `mpsc` channel.
  - Flags: `SQLITE_OPEN_READ_ONLY` when read-only; `READ_WRITE | CREATE` only when `create_if_missing`; otherwise `READ_WRITE`. `SQLITE_OPEN_URI` is never set (a `vfs` cannot sneak in).
  - Extension loading is disabled. `SQLITE_DBCONFIG_DEFENSIVE` is on.
  - `server_info`: `sqlite_version()`.
  - `probe`: `PRAGMA journal_mode`, and whether the file is writable.
  - `ping`: `SELECT 1`.
  - `list_databases` returns `[]`. `tls_summary` returns `encrypted: false, verified: No`.
  - Errors carry the extended result code in `vendor_code`.

### Task 16: Connect pipeline, test connection, `ConnectionManager`, budget, core runtime (HV-CONN-01, -07, CONN-04 subset, D-035)

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/core/hacivat-session/src/{runtime,budget,registry,manager,test_conn}.rs`, `crates/core/hacivat-session/src/pipeline/{mod,validate,steps,trail,interaction}.rs`
- Create: `crates/core/hacivat-session/tests/{pipeline,manager,budget}.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 2's catalog, registry and `sanitize`; Task 10's `classify` and `fixes_for`;
  - Task 4's store and Task 8's `resolve_auth` and `CredentialFiles`;
  - Task 5's `resolve`, `direct`, `classify_host` and `build_tls_params`;
  - `FakeDriver`; Task 1's profile types, `ConnectStep`, `StepCx`, `budget_for` and the driver stubs `PgDriver`, `MySqlDriver`, `SqliteDriver`. Real profiles arrive with Task 12, which merges in the same wave; the tests use `Profile` literals.
- Builds with `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=<main checkout>/target-core/t16` (P14): it compiles the stub driver crates while T13–T15 compile the real ones.
- Produces:
  - **`validate`** (step 0: issues plus DRV-05). Issues: `NameMissing`, `HostMissing`, `PortInvalid`, `FileMissing` (only when not creating), `CreateAndReadOnly`, `TransportUnavailable` for SshTunnel and Karagoz, the TLS file issues, `TlsModeUnsupported { mode: VerifyCa }` for MySQL and MariaDB (P11), and `OptionRejected`.
  - **`connect`**, following HV-CONN-01 steps 0–10, with the trail per R25:
    - CredentialFiles: `PgPass`, shown only when the provider is PgPass.
    - NameResolution: `Resolve`, using the original host. Its addresses go into `Endpoint::Network.resolved`.
    - Transport: `Connect`, a Direct factory, wrapped by `transport_wrap` if set. **With `transport_wrap` set, `Endpoint::Network.direct` is `None`** (P13), which for MySQL means the TCP forwarder. When the driver consumes the factory (`caps.stream_injection`, or `direct` is `None`), this step opens the first stream itself, and its time is the `Connect` step; the factory hands that stream out first, then dials new ones (P23). When the driver dials itself (MySQL Direct), no stream is opened here and the `Connect` step's `elapsed` is `None`, like the TLS step without events.
    - Tls: build the params. **Omitted from the trail when the mode is Disable**, as in the mock's `stepsFor` (P43).
    - Auth: `resolve_auth` on `spawn_blocking`. Time spent in a keyring call or a UI prompt is excluded from the connect timeout.
    - DriverConnect: `Tls` and `SignIn` timings come from `DriverEvent`s. With no events, the TLS step's `elapsed` is `None`.
    - PostConnect: `CheckServer`, `support_status`, `AccessLabel` per R10, the public-plaintext refusal (R3; a second line of defence behind P9), and the `server.unsupported_version` gate (R11).
    - `ConnectDeps.extra_steps` are merged into the ordered list by `order`; a step's returned `CleanupGuard` joins the cleanup stack (P23).
    - `ConnectRequest.department` is passed through for M2b routing and audit; M2a only records it (P44).
  - **Retries (P8): one budget of 3 server rejections per connect gesture.** The pipeline prompts through `UiPort` only for `NeedsPrompt`, when nothing is stored. It re-prompts on `auth.invalid` only when the rejected password came from that prompt, up to 3 rejections in total. A stored or draft password that the server rejects returns `auth.invalid` at once, with `attempts = 1`, so the error dialog can offer re-entry within the same budget. `ConnectError.attempts` counts server rejections. A password that worked is stored with the profile's retention only after success (R8).
  - **On `config.unknown_database`:** a probe connection to `postgres` (then `template1`) for PostgreSQL, or with no database for MySQL, runs `list_databases(50)`. The suggestions are the top 3 by ASCII-case-insensitive edit distance to the requested name; ties go alphabetically.
  - **Cleanup:** the guards run LIFO on failure or cancel, and a late driver result is closed. `connect_timeout` is the profile's value (default 15 s).
  - **`test_connection`:** fresh resources; the median of 3 pings; `probe()` warnings plus `NoTls` and `TlsNotVerified`; never stores anything; takes no budget permit; closes when done.
  - **`ConnectionManager`:**
    - single-flight per profile;
    - one `Budget` per profile (from `max_connections`); the main connection takes one `Priority::Console` permit;
    - a `LiveState` watch, with a generation counter that bumps on every change;
    - a liveness ping every `config.liveness`; a failure sets `Lost { class }`;
    - `disconnect` closes and forgets ForSession secrets; `forget` also cancels;
    - `shutdown` closes everything within `shutdown_budget`; `shutdown_blocking(budget)` does the same from a non-async thread (the GPUI quit handler), waiting on a `std::sync::mpsc` reply with `recv_timeout(budget)`, and returns whether everything closed (P15). A connection that lands after shutdown started is closed at once.
  - **`CoreRuntime::shutdown(&self, budget)`** takes the runtime out of its `Mutex<Option<Runtime>>` and calls `shutdown_timeout(budget)` (P15).
  - **`budget_for(&Profile)`** is `Budget::new(p.max_connections)`.
  - **`default_drivers()`** registers `PgDriver::new()`, `MySqlDriver::new()` and `SqliteDriver::new()` (the T1 stubs in this wave; the real drivers after W3 merges).
  - **Test hygiene (P46, P23):** every test profile's host is an IP literal, `127.0.0.1` for local ones and `203.0.113.10` (TEST-NET-3) for public ones, so no DNS query leaves the machine. The test deps (`deps_with*`, `manager*`) set `transport_wrap` to a stub factory over `tokio::io::duplex`, so no test opens a socket.

```rust
#[tokio::test] async fn budget_of_four_queues_the_fifth_and_serves_priority_first() { let b = Budget::new(4); let held: Vec<_> = (0..4).map(|_| b.try_acquire(Priority::Console).unwrap()).collect();
    assert!(b.try_acquire(Priority::Console).is_none()); let intro = tokio::spawn(b.acquire(Priority::Introspection)); let console = tokio::spawn(b.acquire(Priority::Console));
    drop(held.into_iter().next()); assert!(timeout(ms(50), console).await.is_ok()); assert!(timeout(ms(50), intro).await.is_err()); }
#[test] fn sqlite_budget_is_one() { assert_eq!(budget_for(&sqlite_profile()).max(), 1); assert_eq!(budget_for(&pg_profile(TlsMode::Auto)).max(), 4); }
```

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/app/hacivat-app/src/connections/dialog/{mod,form,url_row,paste}.rs`; `crates/app/hacivat-app/src/connections/mod.rs` (one registration line in the `install` block, appended, P41); `locales/{en,tr}/hacivat-ui.ftl` (block `m2a-t17-dialog`)
- Create: `crates/app/hacivat-app/tests/connection_dialog.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Task 6's primitives, including `TextInput::set_paste_filter`; Task 7's `parse` and `looks_like_connection`;
  - Task 12's `ConnectionsModel`, `apply_parse`, `display_url`, `copy_url`, `suggest_name`, `needs_environment_prompt` and `production_hint`;
  - Task 16's `validate`;
  - the Task 1 stubs `TestStrip` and `ask_environment`, which Task 20 fills in.
- Produces:
  - **`open_connection_dialog(mode, window, cx)`.** A modal layer with id `connection-dialog`, context `ConnectionDialog` and `DialogSize::Form`. Its layout follows NOTES.md surface 1, with the SSH disclosure omitted (R4).
  - **Fields, top to bottom:**
    - URL row (with Copy as URL), Engine Segmented (PostgreSQL / MySQL / MariaDB / SQLite), Name (placeholder = `suggest_name`), Environment Segmented with marks and the hint row, Read-only checkbox.
    - Server section: Host + Port, Database, User. SQLite swaps it for File + Choose… (`PathPromptOptions`) and two checkboxes.
    - Authentication Segmented: Password / pgpass (PostgreSQL only) / Client certificate / None. Password shows the masked field and the Remember Select (4 options with hints; Forever disabled with a hint when `!secrets.keyring_available()`). pgpass shows the matched line, read through `pgpass_lookup` on the core runtime.
    - TLS disclosure: the mode Select with 5 options and hints, the CA file and the client certificate, with summary `hv-conn-tls-sum-auto` or the mode's label. For MySQL and MariaDB, VerifyCa is disabled, with the hint `hv-conn-issue-tls-mode-unsupported` (P11).
    - Advanced disclosure: timeout, keep-alive Select (Off/30/60/300 s), application name, the budget fact, extra properties, with summary `hv-conn-adv-sum`.
    - Below the body: the TestStrip and the footer (Test on the left; Cancel, Save, Save and connect).
  - **Paste (R21):** every field installs a paste filter (`TextInput::set_paste_filter`, P16). The URL field's filter parses every paste; the other text fields' filters parse only when `looks_like_connection` matches; the masked Password field never calls its filter. A recognised paste is consumed and applied through `apply_parse`. Filled fields keep `blue-tint` for 1.6 s, then fade over 240 ms. The InlineNotice lists what happened, as in NOTES §2, with Undo restoring the previous draft. Engine mismatch shows the switch notice.
  - **Save:**
    - An empty Name takes `suggest_name` before `validate`; `NameMissing` is reported only when that is empty too (P30).
    - `validate` issues show inline under their fields, and Save does nothing.
    - If `needs_environment_prompt`, await `ask_environment(name, host, production_hint(host, name), …)`. `None` keeps the dialog open, unchanged.
    - Otherwise `ConnectionsModel::save`, then close and toast `hv-conn-toast-saved`.
    - Save and connect also calls `connect`.
  - **Copy as URL (P31):** puts `copy_url`'s URL on the clipboard and pushes a toast with title `hv-conn-toast-copied`, a data line with the exact URL (`TextSource::Data`), and the line `hv-conn-toast-nopw` when `omitted_password`.
  - **Inline fixes (P40):** on `TestStripEvent::Fix(FixAction)`:
    - `EditConnection` focuses the first field with an issue (Host when none);
    - `TryAgain` re-runs the test;
    - `ReenterPassword` clears and focuses the Password field;
    - `UseDatabase(db)` sets Database and re-runs;
    - `ConnectAnyway` sets the draft's `allow_unsupported_server` and re-runs;
    - `EncryptWithoutVerifying` sets TLS to Require and re-runs;
    - `SupportedVersions` opens R11's URL (`cx.open_url`).
  - **Other behaviour:**
    - Escape, from the dialog or from any field's `InputEvent::Cancelled` (P16): if the TestStrip is running, stop it and stop propagation; otherwise the layer pops.
    - Actions `connection_dialog.{test,save,save_and_connect,copy_url}` are registered with `enabled = a connection dialog is open`, by one line appended to the `connections::install` block (P41).
    - Initial focus is Host.
    - Tab order per NOTES §1, without SSH.

```rust
    emit_strip_fix(&vcx, FixAction::EncryptWithoutVerifying); assert_eq!(draft_tls_mode(&vcx), TlsMode::Require); }

    assert_eq!(env_hint(&vcx), "Asked when you save, because this host isn't on this Mac."); set_field(&vcx, "host", "127.0.0.1");
    assert_eq!(env_hint(&vcx), "Set to local: the server runs on this Mac."); assert_eq!(env_pressed(&vcx), Some(EnvTag::Local)); }

    let vcx = open_new_without_keyring(cx); assert!(remember_option_disabled(&vcx, Retention::Forever)); assert_eq!(remember(&vcx), Retention::UntilRestart); }

    assert_eq!(issue_under(&vcx, "host"), Some("Enter a host.".into())); assert_eq!(layer_ids(&vcx), ["connection-dialog"]); }
#[gpui::test] fn save_local_profile_without_prompt(cx: &mut TestAppContext) { let vcx = open_new(cx, "en"); fill(&vcx, &[("host","localhost"),("database","shop")]);
    vcx.simulate_keystrokes(primary("s")); assert!(layer_ids(&vcx).is_empty()); assert_eq!(profile_names(&vcx), ["shop-local"]); assert_eq!(toast_texts(&vcx), ["Saved shop-local"]); }
#[gpui::test] fn remote_save_waits_for_an_environment(cx: &mut TestAppContext) { let vcx = open_new(cx, "en"); fill(&vcx, &[("host","db.acme.io")]);
    vcx.simulate_keystrokes(primary("s")); assert!(profile_names(&vcx).is_empty()); assert!(layer_ids(&vcx).contains(&"connection-dialog")); }

    assert_eq!(dialog_title(&vcx), "Edit orders-prod"); assert_eq!(dialog_subtitle(&vcx), "Saved · changes apply on the next connect");
    set_remember(&vcx, Retention::Never); vcx.simulate_keystrokes(primary("s")); assert!(keyring_entry(&vcx).is_none()); }
#[gpui::test] fn language_switch_keeps_typed_values(cx: &mut TestAppContext) { let vcx = open_new(cx, "en"); type_into(&vcx, "host", "db.acme.io");
    switch_to(&vcx, "tr"); assert_eq!(field(&vcx, "host"), "db.acme.io"); assert_eq!(dialog_title(&vcx), "Yeni bağlantı"); }
```

### Task 18: Explorer list, sheet, live capsule, status bar, home, commands, delete, pg_service import (NOTES surfaces 7 and 8; R19, R20, R24)

**Files:**
- Modify:
  - `crates/app/hacivat-app/src/connections/{live,capsule,explorer,sheet,status,home,commands,delete,import}.rs`
  - `crates/app/hacivat-app/src/connections/mod.rs` (one registration line in the `install` block, appended)
  - `locales/{en,tr}/hacivat-ui.ftl` (block `m2a-t18-explorer`)
- Create: `crates/app/hacivat-app/tests/{connections_explorer,connections_live}.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 9's capsule, marks, status and explain primitives; Task 10's `PreparedQuery`; Task 12's model; Task 16's manager; Task 3/12's `copy_url`; Task 8's `read_pg_service`; Task 1's hooks, `shell::register_focus_context`, the `connections::boot` wiring and the stubs (`open_connection_dialog`, `open_error_dialog`, `interaction`).
- Produces:
  - **`live::install`** (called from `connections::boot`, never from `install`, P17): sets every shell hook below, and starts a foreground task that awaits `manager.changes()` and mirrors every state into `ConnectionsModel::set_live`. While any profile is `Connecting`, a 50 ms timer repaints the clocks (ticking clocks are the only motion). The capsule clock reads the GPUI executor clock (`cx.background_executor().now()`) captured when the attempt starts, never `Progress.started` (P22). Every hook view renders the M1 element when the `ConnectionsModel` Global is missing (P17).
  - **Explorer (`hooks.explorer`):**
    - The filter field matches through `PreparedQuery` (Task 10). Its `InputEvent::Cancelled` invokes `hooks.escape` (P16).
    - Groups follow `EnvTag::severity_rank`, then unset last (R31), each with a small-caps header and count.
    - Rows, 23 px: engine glyph (`Database`, or `FileCode` for SQLite), name, environment mark, flags (red read-only lock; `LockOpen` when the live connection is plaintext), and engine + major on the right. A live row shows ✓ + latency, the run marker, or ⚠.
    - Selection is `blue-tint` with a 1 px blue edge. The list registers focus context `Explorer` with `shell::register_focus_context(cx, handle, "Explorer")` (R20, P4). ↑/↓ move the selection.
    - The empty state follows NOTES §8.
    - Right-click and the `connection.context_menu` action open a `PopupMenu`: Connect/Disconnect, Edit…, Duplicate, Copy as URL, a separator, then Delete… in red.
  - **Sheet (`hooks.connection_panel`):** kv rows for the selected profile. Access shows `hv-conn-access-ro-guarded` when read-only (R10).
  - **Capsule (`hooks.capsule`), `hooks.capsule_segment` and `hooks.status_left`:** they map `LiveState` for `current()` onto Task 9's models.
    - Body on Failed → `open_error_dialog`.
    - Retry → `connect`.
    - Unencrypted → `Workspace::open_anchored(id, anchor, …)` with the `ExplainPopover`, using `hv-conn-unenc-{title,body,fix}`; `anchor` is the point `SegmentFn` receives (P4).
  - **`hooks.escape`:** cancels the current `Connecting` profile, pushes the `hv-conn-toast-cancelled` toast, and returns true.
  - **`hooks.editor_empty`:** home, with the entry points and their shortcut labels.
  - **Actions:** `connection.{new, paste_url, toggle_connect, edit, duplicate, copy_url, delete, context_menu, import_pg_service}`, each with an `hv-cmd-…` title, registered by one line appended to the `connections::install` block. Only `connection.new` goes into the native File menu, through the File group that Task 1 put in `menus::layout()` (P18); this task does not edit `menus.rs`.
  - **Editing a connecting profile** (RF5, P39): Save in the edit dialog for a profile that is `Connecting` cancels the attempt first, then saves; the next connect uses the new values.
  - **Delete:** an alertdialog that uses `DialogSpec` with a Danger primary and initial focus on Cancel.
  - **pg_service import dialog:** R24.
  - **Quitting (P15):** `on_app_quit` calls `manager.shutdown_blocking(config.shutdown_budget)` synchronously (GPUI waits only 200 ms for quit futures), then `runtime.shutdown(Duration::from_millis(200))` on the `Arc<CoreRuntime>`. A connection that lands during shutdown is closed.
  - **Shots fixtures (R28, P19):** `explorer::{empty_fixture, list_fixture, menu_fixture}`, `delete::confirm_fixture`, `import::pg_service_fixture`, `capsule::{connecting_fixture, connected_unencrypted_fixture, production_ro_fixture, failed_fixture, unsupported_fixture, unencrypted_popover_fixture}`, each `pub fn (window: &mut Window, cx: &mut App)` behind the `shots` feature, over a `ConnectionsBoot::for_tests` shell.
  - New strings: `hv-explorer-group-unset`, `hv-conn-access-ro-guarded`, `hv-capsule-unsupported`, `hv-capsule-clock` (`$seconds (Decimal)`, P28), `hv-conn-enc-unverified`, `hv-conn-toast-cancelled`, and the `hv-conn-import-*` titles and buttons. The empty explorer title reuses M1's `hv-explorer-empty` (P42).

```rust
#[tokio::test] async fn connects_and_detects_every_matrix_target() { for t in targets(None).into_iter().filter(|t| t.id != "pg13" && t.id != "pg17-tr") {
    let live = connect(&deps(), req(profile_for(&t, auto_or_require(&t))), Interaction::Headless, token(), progress()).await.unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{}: {:?}", t.id, e.class));
    assert_eq!(live.server.kind, t.engine, "{}", t.id); assert_eq!(live.support, Support::Supported, "{}", t.id);
    assert_eq!(steps(&live.trail), [Resolve, Connect, Tls, SignIn, CheckServer], "{}", t.id); } }
#[tokio::test] async fn sqlite_file_connects() { assert_eq!(connect_sqlite(tmp_db()).await.server.kind, DbmsKind::Sqlite); }
#[tokio::test] async fn stream_injection_is_used_for_the_main_connection() { for id in ["pg17", "mysql84"] { let (deps, count) = deps_counting();   // transport_wrap set ⇒ direct = None (P13)
    let live = connect(&deps, req(profile_for(&target(id).unwrap(), TlsMode::Require)), Interaction::Headless, token(), progress()).await.unwrap();
    assert!(count.load(Ordering::SeqCst) > 0, "{id}"); assert!(live.trail.iter().any(|s| s.step == Connect), "{id}");
    assert_eq!(live.forwarded(), id == "mysql84", "{id}"); } }   // MySQL goes through the TCP loopback forwarder
#[tokio::test] async fn plaintext_loopback_gets_the_unencrypted_state() { let live = connect_target("pg17", TlsMode::Auto).await; assert!(!live.tls.encrypted); assert_eq!(live.address, AddressClass::Loopback); }
#[tokio::test] async fn test_report_is_complete_for_every_target() { for t in targets(None).into_iter().filter(|t| !matches!(t.id.as_str(), "pg13" | "pg17-tr")) {
    let r = test_target(&t).await; assert!(r.ok, "{}", t.id); assert!(r.ping_median.unwrap() < ms(200)); assert_eq!(r.user.as_deref(), Some("hv_test")); } }
// docker_errors.rs — the helpers use `auto_or_require(&t)` (Require where `tls_default = self_signed`), so TLS never masks the class under test
#[tokio::test] async fn error_classes_against_real_servers() {
    for id in ["pg17", "mysql84", "mariadb114"] { assert_eq!(class_of(id, |p| p.retention(Retention::Never), Some("wrong")).await, ErrClass::AuthInvalid, "{id}");
        let e = err_of(id, |p| p.database("orders_v2")).await; assert_eq!(e.class, ErrClass::ConfigUnknownDatabase, "{id}"); assert_eq!(e.suggestions[0], "orders", "{id}"); }
    assert_eq!(class_of("mysql84", |p| p.user("hv_locked"), None).await, ErrClass::AuthLocked);
    assert_eq!(class_of("pg17", |p| p.port(1), None).await, ErrClass::NetworkRefused);
    assert_eq!(class_of("pg17", |p| p.host("nonexistent.invalid"), None).await, ErrClass::NetworkDns);
    assert_eq!(class_of("pg13", |p| p, None).await, ErrClass::ServerUnsupportedVersion);
    assert_eq!(class_of("pg17-tls", |p| p.tls(TlsMode::VerifyFull), None).await, ErrClass::TlsUntrusted);
}
#[tokio::test] async fn turkish_server_wrong_password_is_auth_invalid() { let e = err_with_pw("pg17-tr", "wrong").await;
    assert_eq!(e.class, ErrClass::AuthInvalid); assert!(e.server.unwrap().text.contains("parola"), "server text must be the verbatim Turkish message"); }
#[tokio::test] async fn encrypt_without_verifying_fix_connects() { let live = connect_target("pg17-tls", TlsMode::Require).await; assert!(live.tls.encrypted); assert_eq!(live.tls.verified, TlsVerified::No); }
// docker_pgpass.rs — 10 vectors (pgpass line set + lookup tuple); outcome from `docker exec m2a-pg17 psql -w` (compose `container_name: m2a-pg17`, P25) with PGPASSFILE vs pgpass_lookup
#[tokio::test] async fn pgpass_parity_with_psql() { for v in vectors() { assert_eq!(ours(&v), psql(&v).await, "{}", v.name); } }
// docker_url.rs
#[tokio::test] async fn url_round_trip_connects() { for id in ["pg17", "mysql84", "mariadb114"] { let p = profile_for(&target(id).unwrap(), TlsMode::Require);
    let url = copy_url(&p).unwrap().url; let ParseResult::Recognised(o) = parse(&url, None) else { panic!("{url}") };
    let mut d = ProfileDraft::new(); apply_parse(&mut d, o, true); d.password = Some("hv_test_pw".into()); d.profile.tls.mode = TlsMode::Require;
    assert!(connect(&deps(), req_draft(d), Interaction::Headless, token(), progress()).await.is_ok(), "{id}"); } }
```

  - **`TestStrip`:**
    - Running: the run marker, `hv-conn-test-running` and a clock (`hv-conn-test-clock`, seconds with 2 decimals), with the trail. The clock reads the GPUI executor clock (`cx.background_executor().now()`) captured when `run` starts, never `Progress.started` (P22).
    - Done/ok: `hv-conn-test-ok` with whole ms, the line "Nothing was saved…", the trail with a duration per step, and the diagnostics rows Server, Latency ("{ms} · median of 3 pings"), Signed in as ("{user} · {method}"), Warnings and Encryption. Copy report sits on the right.
    - Done/failed: the error block, inline.
    - `stop` cancels the `TestHandle`.
  - **The error block:**
    - Title from `error-conn-<class>-title` with `ErrArgs`, never server text. Body from `-body`.
    - The trail with ✕ (R25).
    - Fix buttons from `fixes_for`, mapped to `FixAction`. `UseDatabase` expands into one button per suggestion, at most 3.
    - "Technical details" disclosure: stage, class id, code chip, the server message verbatim (a `TextSource::Data` label), elapsed (3 decimals), and Copy details (English).
  - **`open_error_dialog`:** the same block in a 480 px modal, with id `connection-error`.
  - **`ReenterPassword`** (P8): an inline masked field with "Attempt { $n } of 3" and Connect. The error view keeps a running count of server rejections for this gesture, starting at the error's `attempts` and adding each re-entry's `attempts`; `n` is that count + 1. Once the count reaches 3, the field is removed. Together with the pipeline's rule (Task 16), a gesture never causes more than 3 server rejections.
  - **Fix behaviour:**
    - `ConnectAnyway` saves `allow_unsupported_server = true`, then connects.
    - `EncryptWithoutVerifying` saves `tls.mode = Require`, then connects.
    - `SupportedVersions` opens R11's URL.
  - **`ask_environment`:** a 480 px modal, id `production-prompt`, per NOTES §4.
    - Four choice rows: Production, Staging, Test, Dev. Local is not offered.
    - A preselection from `hint: Option<(&'static str, HintSource)>` (P7), shown with `hv-conn-prod-suggest` ("Suggested because the host name contains “{ $token }”.") for `HintSource::Host`, or `hv-conn-prod-suggest-name` ("Suggested because the name contains “{ $token }”.") for `HintSource::Name`.
    - The consequences list, or the single calm line.
    - The primary is `hv-conn-prod-save` with `$env (Enum(production, staging, test, dev))` (P27), disabled as `hv-conn-prod-pick` when nothing is selected.
    - Enter confirms. Esc sends `None`.
  - **`password_prompt` and `interaction`:** `AppUiPort: UiPort` forwards `Question::Password` through an `mpsc` channel to a foreground task, which opens the password dialog. The answer goes back on a `oneshot`. Cancelling gives `Answer::Cancelled`. `interaction(cx)` returns `Interactive(port)` once installed.
  - **`report`:** the English text of a `TestReport` or `ConnectError`, rendered with the `en` bundle (R26).
  - New strings: `hv-conn-fix-require`, `hv-conn-pw-title`, `hv-conn-pw-body`, `hv-conn-prod-suggest-name`, `hv-conn-test-clock` (`$seconds (Decimal)`, P28), and the `hv-conn-warn-*` warning lines (`NoTls`, `TlsNotVerified`, `NotTested`, `Replica`, `ServerReadOnly`, `SuperReadOnly`, `LowWaitTimeout`, `LocalInfileOn`, `SqliteFileNotWritable`). `hv-capsule-clock` belongs to Task 18's block.
  - **Shots fixtures (R28, P19):** `test_strip::{running_fixture, ok_fixture, failed_fixture}`, `error_view::{auth_fixture, unknown_db_fixture, unsupported_fixture, tls_fixture}` and `prod_prompt::{preselected_fixture, unset_fixture}`, each `pub fn (window: &mut Window, cx: &mut App)` behind the `shots` feature, over a `ConnectionsBoot::for_tests` shell.

| # | Criterion | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| E1 | Connect and test work, through the pipeline in `Auto` (or `Require` where `tls_default = self_signed`), against PostgreSQL 14–18, MySQL 8.0/8.4, MariaDB 10.6/10.11/11.4 and a SQLite file; PG 13 yields `server.unsupported_version` | Task 19 `connects_and_detects_every_matrix_target`, `test_report_is_complete_for_every_target`; resolved versions listed |
| E2 | Stream injection is verified per crate and recorded. PostgreSQL uses the injected stream; MySQL uses the injected stream through the TCP loopback forwarder when a transport wrapper is set, and its own TCP on Direct (P13) | ADR 0002; Task 19 `stream_injection_is_used_for_the_main_connection` |
| E3 | URL round trip: render∘parse is the identity on every catalog template, Copy never contains the password, and pasted URLs connect | Task 7 `every_catalog_template_compiles_and_round_trips`; Task 3 `display_and_copy_never_contain_the_password`; Task 19 `url_round_trip_connects` |
| E4 | Keyring round trip on the PO's Mac, with a dev-signed binary and no prompt on the second run; Delete removes the Keychain item | `live_keyring_round_trip` ×2; demo step 9 |
| E5 | Error-classification goldens: code tables, Fluent EN/TR goldens, and real-server classes including a Turkish server | Task 10 `classify`, `error_fluent_golden`; Task 19 `error_classes_against_real_servers`, `turkish_server_wrong_password_is_auth_invalid` |
| E6 | pgpass parity with `psql` on 10 vectors | Task 19 `pgpass_parity_with_psql` |
| E7 | Shots are clean across en/tr/en-XA/tr-XT × dark/light; the PO signs off the design | Task 21 harness, 0 violations; PO sign-off |
| E8 | Gates are green: clippy `-D warnings`, the full test set, `check-deps`, `l10n check` (with L026), `tokens --check`, `cargo deny`, and CI including `docker-pr` | the Step 2 output; the CI run link |
| E9 | No secret at rest outside the keyring | `connections_toml_never_contains_a_secret`; `grep -r hv_test_pw ~/Library/Application\ Support/<app>` after the demo returns nothing |
| E10 | Budgets hold: first frame ≤ 500 ms and idle RSS ≤ 200 MB with no connection; idle RSS with one connection recorded | `--smoke` ×3 |

## Self-review notes

- **Spec coverage (L15 M2a and the §4 rows):**
  - HV-DRV-01 → T2, T13–15. HV-DRV-02/03 → T3. HV-DRV-04 → T7. HV-DRV-05 → T2, T16 (Validate), T18 (import).
  - Stream injection with the forwarder fallback → T5, T13, T14, T19 and ADR 0002.
  - HV-CONN-01 → T16. HV-CONN-03 → T10, T16, T20. HV-CONN-07 → T16, T20.
  - HV-CONN-04 subset → T16 and T18 (R12). HV-CONN-02 and -05 are deferred with reasons (R12).
  - HV-SEC-03 → T8, T16. HV-SEC-04 → T8, T18, T19. HV-SEC-05 → T4, T12. The vault interface → T1/T4 (R5).
  - TLS, M2a parts → T5, T13, T14, T16, T18 (badge) (R3).
  - D-004 → T12 rules and T20 prompt. D-005 → R3. D-035 → T16 and the Advanced fact (R13). D-016 → R5. D-036 is untouched (no transactions in M2a). D-085 → M2b.
  - Safety and read-only labels and seams → T16 and T18 (R10).
  - Every UI surface in NOTES.md that M2a ships → T6, T9, T17, T18, T20. The deliberately omitted ones are listed in R4.
  - Tests:
    - unit tests per crate (T2–T18);
    - the Docker harness (T11) and the conformance suite (T19);
    - pseudo-locale shots (T21);
    - the minimal CI Docker job (T11);
    - the gate (T22, criteria E1–E10).
- **M1 deferred items:** the `(Number)`→ExactDecimal codegen → T10 as `(Decimal)` (R15). `PreparedQuery` → T10 (P14). jiff → already a prerequisite; no instants in M2a. Demo status-bar mismatch → fixed by T18's status hook. Native menu enablement snapshot → avoided by R20.
- **M1 lessons applied:** the shared target dirs (Execution); worktrees start on `main` (the `checkout -B` first step); append-only files (the list in Execution); never a plain `cargo update` (Dependencies); the strong-entity rule for custom layers (Execution, and T17/T20 subscribe before push); settings via `update_settings` and profiles via `ConnectionsModel`.
- **Step scan:**
  - Every test step lists names and assertions with exact values.
  - Every code step names the signature and the values the spec pins.
  - Bodies appear only for exact copy and test vectors.
  - Helpers named in tests (`open_new`, `with_profiles`, `targets_of`, `ok`, `fv`, …) are written by the task that owns the test file; shared app helpers are appended to `tests/support/mod.rs`.
- **Type consistency:**
  - `ErrClass`, `FixId`, `AccessLabel` and `Origin` are from `hacivat-proto` everywhere.
  - `Phase` lives in `hacivat-driver::errors` and is re-exported by `hacivat-session::pipeline`.
  - `TlsMode` and `TlsSettings` are from `hacivat-net::tls` everywhere.
  - `Retention`, `SecretSlot` and `AuthProviderId` are from `hacivat-secrets`.
  - `validate` is owned by T16 (pipeline step 0) and consumed by T17. It is not in T12, which avoids a same-wave dependency.
  - `copy_url`, `apply_parse` and `production_hint` (not `suggests_production`) are owned by T12. `production_hint` returns `Option<(&'static str, HintSource)>`, and `ask_environment`'s `hint` has the same type (T1, T12, T17, T20).
  - `TestStrip::{run, stop, state}`, `ask_environment(name, host, hint, …)` and `open_error_dialog(id, error, …)` match between Task 1, T17, T18, T20 and T21.
  - `SegmentFn` and `Capsule::on_segment` both carry `Point<Pixels>` (T1, T9, T18). `shell::register_focus_context(cx, …)` is a free function (T1, T18).
  - `CoreRuntime::shutdown(&self, budget)` and `ConnectionManager::shutdown_blocking(&self, budget) -> bool` (T1, T16, T18).
  - `TextInput::set_paste_filter` (T1 signature, T6 body, T17 and T18 callers).
  - `LoopbackForwarder::bind(factory, dir, max_accepts, mode)` (T1, T5, T14); `Endpoint::Network { factory, direct, resolved, tls_host }` (T1, T13, T14, T16).
  - `ProfileDraft::{new, edit}`, `MemoryBackend::{new, with_max_blob}`, `EngineCatalog::engines`, `Template::default_of`, `budget_for`, `ParseResult::unrecognised`, `KeyringBackend::persistent` and the three driver stubs are declared in T1.
  - The `CapsuleModel::Connection` fields match between Task 1, T9 and T18.
  - The keymap command IDs match between Task 1, T17 and T18.
- **Review Focus coverage:** each of the five items has its tests in the owning task (#1 T3, T7, T12, T16, T17; #2 T12; #3 T2, T7, T10, T12, T19; #4 T4, T12, T16; #5 T16, T18, T21).
- **Proportion:** the plan defines signatures, rules and test assertions. The only full listings are test vectors and the exact copy and values from `alg/01` and NOTES.md.

### Pre-flight conflict scan (same-wave pairs that share a crate, a file, or an interface)

| Wave | Pair | Shared surface | Risk | Resolution |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| W1 | T2 × T3 | crate `hacivat-driver`; catalog templates (T2) vs the template grammar (T3) | Same-hash concurrent builds; T2 writes templates that T3's compiler rejects | **One agent, T2 then T3** (P14), in `target-core/t02-t03`. T3 tests use its own template strings. The catalog-wide compile and round-trip test is in T7 (W2). |
| W1 | T4 × T5 × (T2→T3) | `hacivat-net` compiled as a dependency | A binary links another worktree's stub `hacivat-net` | Per-task target dirs `target-core/{t02-t03,t04,t05}` (P14). |
| W1 | T6 × (M1 code) | `dialog.rs`, `segmented.rs`, `input/mod.rs`, `menu.rs` | M1 tests break; `Segmented` tab stops change in the app | T6 runs `cargo test -p hacivat-ui` in full and `cargo test -p hacivat-app --features scene-check,pseudo` (Step 4). `DialogSize` literals were already updated by Task 1. |
| W2 | T7 × T10 | crate `hacivat-driver` (url vs errors) | `lib.rs` module lines | Task 1 declared every module; neither task touches `lib.rs`. |
| W2 | T10 × T11 | crate `xtask` | `main.rs` dispatch | L026 lives inside `l10n/{rules,mod,smoke}.rs` (no new arm). Only T11 appends the `docker` arm. T10 builds in the gpui `target`, T11 in `target-core`. |
| W2 | T9 × T10 | `crates/app` (`layers.rs` vs `settings_ui/mod.rs`); `hacivat-l10n` built in the shared gpui `target` | Same-hash `hacivat-l10n` | Disjoint files. Both run `cargo clean -p hacivat-l10n -p hacivat-app` before final verification. |
| W2 | T10 × (M1) | `hacivat-l10n/src/search.rs` | Search regressions | T10 appends `PreparedQuery` (moved from T18, P14) and runs the existing search tests; the equivalence test pins it. |
| W2 | T9 × T6 (merged) | `capsule.rs`/`EnvKind` used by T6's `ChoiceList` marks | Mark rendering differs | `ChoiceList` calls `env_swatch`, which T9 owns; T6 shipped against Task 1's stub swatch. |
| W3 | T12 × T16 | crate `hacivat-session` (`profile/**` vs `pipeline/**`, `manager`) | `validate` placement; Profile semantics | `validate` is owned by T16. T16 tests build `Profile` literals and do not need T12's store. |
| W3 | T12 (app model) × T16 (manager) | the `ConnectionManager` API | The model is coded against a stub | The signatures are fixed in Task 1. Live mirroring is tested in T18 (W4), after T16 merges. |
| W3 | T13 × T14 × T15 × T16 | driver crates built as stubs (T16) and for real (T13–T15) | Stub/real cross-linking | Per-task `target-core/<task>` (P14). |
| W3 | T13 × T14 × T15 | `docs/decisions/0002-driver-crates.md`; `Cargo.lock` | Merge conflicts | One append-only `## m2a-tNN — <crate>` section per task. No manifest or `deny.toml` edits (Task 1 wrote both). |
| W3 | T13 × T14 | Docker services (shared ports) | Parallel `up`/`down`; recreation from another worktree | Only the controller runs `docker up`/`down`. `xtask docker test` never runs `up` and fails fast on an unhealthy service (P25). |
| W4 | T17 × T20 | `TestStrip` embedding, `ask_environment` | T17 tests depend on T20 behaviour | T17 tests only stub-invariant behaviour (`remote_save_waits_for_an_environment`). The joint flows are in T21. |
| W4 | T17 × T18 | `open_connection_dialog` called by T18's commands | A T18 test asserting the dialog would fail on the Task 1 stub | T18's tests never assert the dialog layer. `cmd_e_opens_the_edit_dialog` is in T21, after both merge. Merge order within W4 is free. |
| W4 | T17 × T18 × T20 | `open_error_dialog`, `interaction()`, `open_connection_dialog`; the `connections::install` block | — | Task 1 stubs; one append-only registration line each (T17's registers `connection_dialog.*`, P41); hooks are set only by `live::install` from `boot` (P17); the joint flows are in T21. |
| W4 | T19 × all | Docker matrix | Flaky server start | T19 only reads; the controller holds the compose project up for W4. |
| W4 | T17 × T18 × T20 | `locales/{en,tr}/hacivat-ui.ftl` | Duplicate IDs | Append-only blocks; the ID prefixes split by task (`hv-conn-dlg-*`, `hv-conn-issue-*`, `hv-conn-url-*`, `hv-conn-toast-{saved,copied,nopw}` = T17; `hv-explorer-*`, `hv-conn-menu-*`, `hv-conn-del-*`, `hv-conn-import-*`, `hv-capsule-*`, `hv-home-*`, `hv-status-env`, `hv-conn-toast-cancelled`, `hv-conn-access-*`, `hv-conn-enc-*`, `hv-conn-unenc-*` = T18; `hv-conn-err-*`, `hv-conn-fix-*`, `hv-conn-prod-*`, `hv-conn-test-*`, `hv-conn-diag-*`, `hv-conn-step-*`, `hv-conn-warn-*`, `hv-conn-pw-*` = T20; already merged: `hv-menu-file` = T1, `hv-conn-file-*`, `hv-conn-keyring-*`, `hv-conn-store-*`, `hv-conn-toast-dup` = T12). `l10n check` L005 catches any duplicate at merge. |

## Appendix: pre-flight rulings applied (M2a)

Source: `<main checkout>/.superpowers/sdd/2026-11-12-m2a-connect/preflight.md`, Part 3. All rulings P1–P49 adopted by the controller on 2026-11-12, together with four binding controller rulings: whole-token production hint with `HintSource` (P7); MySQL trust via `with_disable_built_in_roots` plus OS roots as DER from `rustls-native-certs`, with the scoped `webpki-roots` exception written in T1 (P1, P10); a T4 test that fails on keyring's mock backend, with the five keyring features; MySQL never on the Unix-socket path, `prefer_socket(false)` (P10).
